<script>
  $('body').imagesLoaded(function() {
   $('.follow-on-scroll').followingContainer();
  });

    $(document).ready(function() {
      $(".index-carousel").lightSlider({
        gallery: true,
        item: 1,
        loop:true,
        thumbItem: 0,
        pager: false,
        slideMargin: 0,
        auto: true, 
        pause: 3000,
        pauseOnHover: true
      }); 

      $('.masonry-event-box').imagesLoaded( function() {
        $('.masonry-event-box').masonry();
      });

      $('[data-more_ttd]').each(function() {
        if($(this).parent().parent().find('.index-event-item-container').data('total') > $(this).parent().parent().find('.index-event-item').length)
          $(this).show();
       });

      $('[data-more_ttd]').click(function(event) {
        event.preventDefault();

        var offset = 0;
        $container = $(this).parent().parent().find('.index-event-item-container');
        $btn = $(this);

        $btn.data('original-text', $btn.html());

        $btn.html('Loading...');

        $.get('/_ajax/TtdJSONRequest.php', { method: $(this).data('method'), date: $(this).data('date') }).done(function(data) {
        
          var response = $.parseJSON(data);
          var iteration = 0; 

          var html = '<div class="index-event-item-container"> \
                        <h2 class="index-heading">' + $btn.data('heading') + '</h2>';

          $.each(response, function(val, item) {

            iteration = iteration + 1;

            if(iteration <= offset)
              return;

            $btn.html($btn.data('original-text'));

            html += '<div class="index-event-item index-ttd-item">\
                          <figure class="index-event-item-image"> \
                            <div style="background-image: url(' + item.image + ')"></div> \
                          </figure> \
                          <div class="index-event-item-content"> \
                            <h3>' + item.name + '</h3> \
                            ' + item.description + ' \
                          </div> \
                        </div>';

          });

          html += '</div>';

        $.fancybox.open({
          src : html,
            type: 'html',
            opts : { 
            toolbar: false,
            smallBtn: true,
            baseClass: 'ttd-items-popup'
          }});
          
        });

      });

    });

  </script>

    $('#load-upcomming-weekend-events').click(function(event) {
    
      event.preventDefault();

    $('body').on('click', '.load-more', function() {
      var element = $(this).parent().parent();
      var button = $(this);
      $(this).html('<i class="fa fa-spin fa-spinner"></i> Loading...</i>');
      $.get( "/_scripts/Events/controller.php", { action: 'loadmore', type: $(this).data('type'), date: $(this).data('date'), offset: $(this).data('offset') } )
      .done(function( data ) {
        button.parent().before('<div class="ajax-data pure-g regular-padding" style="display: none">' + data + '</div>');
        $('.ajax-data').fadeIn('slow');
        button.fadeOut();
      });
    });
  
    $('body').on('click', 'h1.date', function(event) {
      event.preventDefault();
      var target = $(this).next();
      target.slideToggle();
      if($(this).find('.minimize').html() == '[ - ]')
        $(this).find('.minimize').html('[ + ]');
      else 
        $(this).find('.minimize').html('[ - ]');
    });

      $('.left-filter').slideToggle();

      $('#popup-search').toggle();

      var open = $(this).data('view');
      $('.slider-tabs > *').addClass('inactive');

      $('.tabbed-slider-content').hide();
      $('#' + open).show();

      $(this).removeClass('inactive');
    });
